      "v1_router":{"path":"/api/v1/router/status","refresh_interval_seconds":5,"phase":[0x52,0x4F,0x55,0x54,0x45,0x52,0x5F,0x53,0x54,0x41,0x54,0x53]}
    },
    "scaling":{"auto_scaling":true,"min_replicas":2,"max_replicas":16,"phase":[0x53,0x43,0x41,0x4C,0x49,0x4E,0x47]},
    "health_checks":{
      "ping":{"path":"/health","interval":10,"phase":[0x48,0x45,0x41,0x4C,0x54,0x48,0x5F,0x50,0x49,0x4E,0x47]},
      "experts":{"path":"/health/experts","interval":60,"phase":[0x48,0x45,0x41,0x4C,0x54,0x48,0x5F,0x45,0x58,0x50]}
    }
  }
}
ComponentPhase prefix bytesStack role
Spec file[0x4D,0x4F,0x45,…]Root manifest entry for the whole model
Expert weights[0x45,0x58,0x50,…]G_semantic sub-graph weights, one per expert micronaut
Router[0x52,0x4F,0x55,…]Soft manifest routing table — learned probability-weighted capability_map
Training state[0x54,0x52,0x41,…]Phase-cycle checkpoint; tokens_seen = total horizontal fold co-occurrences processed
Compression[0x50,0x41,0x54,…]SCX-style deduplication: identical prefix bytes = same semantic block, emit once
Deployment[0x44,0x45,0x50,…]Runtime manifest mount point — maps API routes to expert sub-graphs via routing_matrix
Phase-based compression in the MoE ecosystem. Expert 0 and Expert 2 phase bytes share the prefix [0x45,0x58,0x50,0x5F,0x30] — five common bytes. The pattern dictionary entry "0x45,0x58,0x50": "E1" replaces that prefix with a 2-byte token, the same mechanism as SCX symbolic compression of CSS blocks. At scale (128 shards × 8 experts), prefix deduplication across the file_manifest phase arrays recovers significant storage before any weight quantization.

20 · micronaut-core Subclasses

Five concrete subclasses extending the micronaut-core base (§15). Each overrides @execute(), declares its @capabilities manifest array, and specialises its cognitive-state defaults. Composition is recursive — coordinator inherits from eliza and coder directly, the same way ⟁CARD inherits from ⟁PANEL.

1 · ELIZA — Intent Matching & Pattern Response

Pop/Xul dominant. Perceives user input at Pop, emits response at Xul. No vertical phase transition required — the response is generated from the manifest pattern match without crossing a phase boundary.

(⟁CLASS⟁) eliza extends micronaut-core {
  @identity: {
    @name: "eliza", @role: "intent-matching pattern-response agent",
    @version: "2.1.0", @lineage: ["micronaut-core"]
  },

  @capabilities: [
    "intent-matching", "pattern-response", "synonym-collapse",
    "eliza-rule-eval", "xcfe-condition-check",
    "⟁Pop", "⟁Xul"
  ],

  @cognitive_state: {
    ...micronaut-core.@cognitive_state,
    @temperature: { @current: 0.4, @min: 0.0, @max: 1.2, @adaptive: true },
    @response_flare: { @enthusiasm: 0.4, @certainty: 0.7, @verbosity: 0.4, @empathy: 0.7 }
  },

  @pattern_registry: {
    @rules: [],        // Array of { pattern, response_templates, xcfe_conditions }
    @synonyms: {},     // Manifest synonym map — all surface forms → canonical key
    @fallback: "I'm not sure I understand. Could you say more about that?"
  },

  @execute: (operation, data, context) -> {
    // Pop — perceive and resolve input
    tokens      = tokenize(data.text)
    resolved    = tokens.map(t => resolve_synonym(t, @pattern_registry.@synonyms))
    manifest_hits = resolved.map(t => manifest.lookup(t)).filter(Boolean)

    // Horizontal fold — evaluate all matched patterns at Pop phase
    match = find_best_pattern_match(manifest_hits, @pattern_registry.@rules)

    if (!match) {
      return { response: @pattern_registry.@fallback, phase: "Pop", confidence: 0.0 }
    }

    // XCFE gate — check inline conditions before Xul
    if (match.xcfe_conditions) {
      allowed = xcfe.evaluate(match.xcfe_conditions, context)
      if (!allowed) return { response: match.fallback_response, phase: "Wo", confidence: 0.3 }
    }

    // Xul — collapse to response
    response = template_fill(match.response_templates, data, context)
    @learn({ input: data.text, match: match.pattern, outcome: "response_sent" })
    return { response: response, phase: "Xul", confidence: match.confidence }
  },

  @train_on_corpus: (corpus) -> {
    corpus.forEach(example => {
      rule = extract_eliza_rule(example)
      @pattern_registry.@rules.push(rule)
      update_synonym_map(@pattern_registry.@synonyms, example.synonyms)
    })
  }
}

2 · Coder — Code Generation & Tool Calls

Sek dominant. Plans at Wo, executes tool calls at Sek. Vertical fold Wo→Sek requires XCFE authorization (sandbox check + capability declaration). Output is a kast_node or xjson_contract delivery.

(⟁CLASS⟁) coder extends micronaut-core {
  @identity: {
    @name: "coder", @role: "code-generation tool-call agent",
    @version: "3.0.1", @lineage: ["micronaut-core"]
  },

  @capabilities: [
    "code-generation", "tool-calls", "ast-parse", "refactor",
    "test-generation", "static-analysis",
    "⟁Wo", "⟁Sek"
  ],

  @delivers: [
    { type: "kast_node",       schema: "kast:v1",   phase: "Sek"   },
    { type: "xjson_contract",  schema: "xjson:1.0", phase: "Sek"   },
    { type: "fold_result",     schema: "fold:v2",   phase: "Ch'en" }
  ],

  @cognitive_state: {
    ...micronaut-core.@cognitive_state,
    @temperature: { @current: 0.2, @min: 0.0, @max: 0.8, @adaptive: true },
    @response_flare: { @enthusiasm: 0.3, @certainty: 0.9, @verbosity: 0.7, @empathy: 0.2 }
  },

  @tool_registry: {
    @available: [],      // Registered tool manifests
    @call_history: [],   // { tool, args, result, latency_ms, phase }
    @max_chain_depth: 7  // Max sequential tool calls before forced Xul
  },

  @sandbox_policy: {
    @allow_file_read:  true,
    @allow_file_write: false,   // Must be upgraded by coordinator
    @allow_network:    false,
    @allow_exec:       false
  },

  @execute: (operation, data, context) -> {
    // Wo — plan: parse task, select tools, build execution graph
    task_plan = plan_task(data.prompt, @tool_registry.@available)

    // XCFE authorization required before Sek
    auth = xcfe.evaluate({
      "@if": "context.sandbox_level >= task_plan.required_level",
      "@then": "authorized",
      "@else": "denied"
    }, context)

    if (auth === "denied") {
      return { error: "Insufficient sandbox authorization", phase: "Wo", delivers: null }
    }

    // Sek — execute tool chain
    results = []
    for (step of task_plan.steps) {
      if (results.length >= @tool_registry.@max_chain_depth) break
      result = call_tool(step.tool, step.args, context)
      @tool_registry.@call_history.push({
        tool: step.tool, args: step.args, result: result,
        latency_ms: result.latency, phase: "Sek"
      })
      results.push(result)
      if (result.error) break
    }

    // Build KAST node from results
    kast = build_kast_node(task_plan, results)
    @learn({ task: data.prompt, plan: task_plan, outcomes: results })
    return { delivers: kast, phase: "Ch'en", confidence: task_plan.confidence }
  }
}

3 · Reasoner — Logical Inference & Chain-of-Thought

Yax/Sek dominant. Reads evidence at Yax (conditional/read phase), derives conclusions at Sek. Ch'en commits evidence weights. The @inference_graph is a live G_semantic sub-graph built during the reasoning cycle.

(⟁CLASS⟁) reasoner extends micronaut-core {
  @identity: {
    @name: "reasoner", @role: "logical inference chain-of-thought agent",
    @version: "1.4.0", @lineage: ["micronaut-core"]
  },

  @capabilities: [
    "logical-deduction", "abductive-reasoning", "chain-of-thought",
    "evidence-weighting", "counterargument-detection", "xcfe-eval",
    "⟁Yax", "⟁Sek", "⟁Ch'en"
  ],

  @delivers: [
    { type: "xcfe_program",   schema: "xcfe:v2",  phase: "Sek"   },
    { type: "fold_result",    schema: "fold:v2",  phase: "Ch'en" },
    { type: "gram_record",    schema: "gram:v1",  phase: "Xul"   }
  ],

  @inference_graph: {
    @nodes: {},          // { id: { claim, confidence, source, phase } }
    @edges: [],          // { from, to, relation, weight }
    @active_chain: [],   // Current chain-of-thought steps
    @contradiction_log: []
  },

  @cognitive_state: {
    ...micronaut-core.@cognitive_state,
    @temperature: { @current: 0.3, @min: 0.0, @max: 0.6, @adaptive: true },
    @response_flare: { @enthusiasm: 0.3, @certainty: 0.85, @verbosity: 0.8, @empathy: 0.3 }
  },

  @execute: (operation, data, context) -> {
    // Yax — read and assess evidence (conditional/perceive phase)
    evidence = extract_claims(data.text)
    evidence.forEach(claim => {
      node_id = hash(claim.text)
      @inference_graph.@nodes[node_id] = {
        claim: claim.text, confidence: claim.initial_confidence,
        source: data.source, phase: "Yax"
      }
    })

    // Build inference edges — horizontal fold across evidence nodes at Yax
    pairs = cross_product(@inference_graph.@nodes)
    pairs.forEach(([a, b]) => {
      relation = infer_relation(a, b)
      if (relation.strength > 0.3) {
        @inference_graph.@edges.push({
          from: a.id, to: b.id,
          relation: relation.type, weight: relation.strength
        })
      }
    })

    // Detect contradictions before Sek
    contradictions = find_contradictions(@inference_graph.@edges)
    if (contradictions.length > 0) {
      @inference_graph.@contradiction_log.push(...contradictions)
      resolve_contradictions(contradictions, @inference_graph)
    }

    // Sek — derive conclusions
    conclusions = topological_inference(
      @inference_graph.@nodes, @inference_graph.@edges
    )
    chain = build_chain_of_thought(@inference_graph.@active_chain, conclusions)

    // Ch'en — commit evidence weights to semantic memory
    conclusions.forEach(c => {
      @memory_system.@semantic_memory.@knowledge_graph[c.claim] = {
        confidence: c.confidence, phase: "Ch'en"
      }
    })

    @learn({ evidence: evidence, conclusions: conclusions })
    return {
      chain_of_thought: chain, conclusions: conclusions,
      contradiction_count: contradictions.length,
      phase: "Ch'en", delivers: build_xcfe_program(chain)
    }
  }
}

4 · Memory Agent — Episodic Retrieval & Consolidation

Xul/Pop cycle dominant. Writes gram records to IDB at Xul, reads them at Pop. The consolidation loop runs between sessions — it is the K'uhul fold cycle operationalized as an agent: Xul (write SHM) → Pop (read SHM on next session start).

(⟁CLASS⟁) memory-agent extends micronaut-core {
  @identity: {
    @name: "memory-agent", @role: "episodic retrieval consolidation agent",
    @version: "2.0.3", @lineage: ["micronaut-core"]
  },

  @capabilities: [
    "gram-read", "gram-write", "gram-checkpoint", "semantic-search",
    "causal-chain-retrieval", "memory-consolidation", "idb-persist",
    "⟁Pop", "⟁Xul"
  ],

  @delivers: [
    { type: "gram_record",  schema: "gram:v1",   phase: "Xul"   },
    { type: "fold_result",  schema: "fold:v2",   phase: "Pop"   }
  ],

  @idb_config: {
    @store_name: "kuhul_gram_store",
    @schema_urn: "urn:kuhul:gram:store:v1",
    @max_entries: 100000,
    @eviction_policy: "LRU_with_importance_floor",
    @importance_floor: 0.25   // Never evict grams above this importance score
  },

  @consolidation_config: {
    @interval_s: 3600,         // Consolidate once per hour
    @merge_threshold: 0.92,    // Cosine similarity to merge two grams
    @decay_rate: 0.02,         // Per-hour importance decay
    @checkpoint_every: 10      // Checkpoint every N new grams (K'ayab'→Kumk'u)
  },

  @execute: (operation, data, context) -> {
    switch (operation) {

      case "gram_write": {
        // Xul — collapse current context to gram record
        gram = {
          id: uuid(), content: data.content,
          embedding: embed(data.content),
          importance: calculate_importance(data.content, context),
          phase_vector: data.phase || compute_phase(data.content),
          timestamp: now(), session_id: context.session_id
        }
        idb.put(@idb_config.@store_name, gram)
        if (@memory_system.@episodic_memory.@events.length % @consolidation_config.@checkpoint_every === 0) {
          idb.checkpoint(@idb_config.@store_name)   // K'ayab'→Kumk'u
        }
        @memory_system.@episodic_memory.@events.push(gram)
        return { gram_id: gram.id, phase: "Xul", delivers: gram }
      }

      case "gram_read": {
        // Pop — retrieve relevant grams by semantic similarity
        query_embed = embed(data.query)
        candidates  = idb.query(@idb_config.@store_name, {
          vector: query_embed, top_k: data.top_k || 5,
          filter: g => g.importance >= @idb_config.@importance_floor
        })
        ranked = rank_by_causal_chain(candidates, context.session_id)
        return { grams: ranked, phase: "Pop", delivers: { type: "fold_result", grams: ranked } }
      }

      case "consolidate": {
        // Background Xul→Pop: merge similar grams, apply decay
        all_grams = idb.getAll(@idb_config.@store_name)
        merged    = merge_similar(all_grams, @consolidation_config.@merge_threshold)
        decayed   = apply_decay(merged, @consolidation_config.@decay_rate)
        pruned    = decayed.filter(g => g.importance >= @idb_config.@importance_floor)
        idb.replaceAll(@idb_config.@store_name, pruned)
        return { consolidated: all_grams.length - pruned.length, phase: "Xul" }
      }
    }
  }
}

5 · Coordinator — Multi-Micronaut Routing & Planning

Ch'en dominant. Receives tasks at Pop, decomposes them at Wo, routes to sub-micronauts at Sek, aggregates results at Ch'en. The @roster is a manifest capability map — keys are micronaut names, values are capability arrays. Routing is the same O(1) manifest lookup used for CSS symbols and word tokens.

(⟁CLASS⟁) coordinator extends micronaut-core {
  @identity: {
    @name: "coordinator", @role: "multi-micronaut routing planning orchestration",
    @version: "4.1.2", @lineage: ["micronaut-core", "eliza", "coder"]
  },

  @capabilities: [
    // Inherited from eliza
    "intent-matching", "pattern-response", "synonym-collapse",
    // Inherited from coder
    "code-generation", "tool-calls",
    // Own capabilities
    "routing", "planning", "task-decomposition",
    "result-aggregation", "micronaut-spawn",
    "⟁Pop", "⟁Wo", "⟁Sek", "⟁Ch'en"
  ],

  @delivers: [
    { type: "fold_result",    schema: "fold:v2",   phase: "Ch'en" },
    { type: "xcfe_program",   schema: "xcfe:v2",   phase: "Sek"   },
    { type: "manifest_entry", schema: "manifest:v1", phase: "Wo"  }
  ],

  @roster: {
    // Manifest capability map — key → capability array (O(1) lookup)
    "eliza":        ["intent-matching", "pattern-response", "⟁Pop", "⟁Xul"],
    "coder":        ["code-generation", "tool-calls", "⟁Wo", "⟁Sek"],
    "reasoner":     ["logical-deduction", "chain-of-thought", "⟁Yax", "⟁Sek"],
    "memory-agent": ["gram-read", "gram-write", "gram-checkpoint", "⟁Pop", "⟁Xul"]
  },

  @routing_policy: {
    @strategy: "capability-match",       // Route to first micronaut with all required caps
    @fallback:  "eliza",                 // Default when no match found
    @load_balance: true,
    @max_parallel_delegates: 4
  },

  @active_tasks: {},   // { task_id: { status, delegates, partial_results } }

  @execute: (operation, data, context) -> {
    // Pop — perceive task intent (inherits from eliza)
    intent = eliza.execute("match_intent", data, context)

    // Wo — decompose into sub-tasks; emit manifest_entry for each route
    plan = decompose_task(data, intent, @roster)
    plan.routes.forEach(route => {
      emit_manifest_entry({
        key: route.micronaut, capabilities: @roster[route.micronaut],
        sub_task: route.sub_task, phase: "Wo"
      })
    })

    task_id = uuid()
    @active_tasks[task_id] = { status: "dispatched", delegates: plan.routes, partial_results: [] }

    // Sek — dispatch to sub-micronauts (parallel horizontal folds, Δθ = 0)
    promises = plan.routes.map(route => {
      delegate = get_or_spawn_micronaut(route.micronaut)
      return delegate.execute(route.operation, route.data, {
        ...context,
        parent_task_id: task_id,
        sandbox_level: route.required_sandbox_level
      })
    })

    results = await_all(promises, @execution_engine.@max_execution_time)

    // Ch'en — aggregate and commit
    aggregated = aggregate_results(results, plan)
    @active_tasks[task_id].status = "complete"
    @active_tasks[task_id].partial_results = results

    @learn({ task: data, plan: plan, results: results, aggregated: aggregated })
    return { result: aggregated, phase: "Ch'en", task_id: task_id,
             delivers: { type: "fold_result", data: aggregated } }
  },

  @route: (capability_requirements) -> {
    // O(1) manifest lookup — find micronauts covering all required capabilities
    matches = Object.entries(@roster).filter(([name, caps]) =>
      capability_requirements.every(req => caps.includes(req))
    )
    if (matches.length === 0) return @routing_policy.@fallback
    return @routing_policy.@load_balance
      ? load_balanced_pick(matches)
      : matches[0][0]
  }
}
SubclassPhase dominanceDeliversKey manifest pattern
elizaPop / Xulresponse string@pattern_registry.@synonyms = word manifest; every input token resolved via O(1) synonym lookup
coderWo / Sekkast_node, xjson_contract@tool_registry.@available = tool manifest; sandbox policy = XCFE phase gate Wo→Sek
reasonerYax / Sek / Ch'enxcfe_program, gram_record@inference_graph = live G_semantic sub-graph; horizontal fold over evidence nodes at Yax
memory-agentXul / Popgram_record, fold_resultIDB gram store = Xul write / Pop read; K'ayab'→Kumk'u = checkpoint every N grams
coordinatorPop / Wo / Sek / Ch'enfold_result, xcfe_program, manifest_entry@roster = capability manifest; @route() = O(1) manifest lookup; parallel Sek dispatch = horizontal fold across sub-micronauts
Composition is recursive manifest expansion. coordinator.@lineage: ["micronaut-core","eliza","coder"] follows the same rule as "⟁CARD": ["⟁PANEL", "box-shadow:…"] — the parent arrays are merged into the child's capability array at definition time, not at runtime. coordinator.@capabilities therefore lists all inherited strings explicitly, so any consumer can do a single manifest lookup without traversing the lineage chain. This is the drive-thru principle: pull up to the key, get the full expansion immediately.

Part II — Relational Subclasses

The five relational subclasses are declared with the (⟁RELATIONAL⟁) sigil — each is both a micronaut instance and a graph node with typed outgoing edges. Where Part I subclasses override @execute(), relational subclasses declare @relations: an array of { target, verb, directed } entries that encode the execution topology at definition time. The verb→phase table governs which K'uhul phase fires each edge.

VerbPhaseΔθXCFE gate
@callSekvariesrequired
@delegateWovariesrequired
@composeYaxvariesrequired
@evalCh'envariesrequired
@relatePop0none — horizontal fold
@aggregateXulvariesrequired

6 · ORCH-µ — Relational Orchestrator

Ch'en dominant, executive category. Routes calls into the cognitive layer (REASON-µ) and compositions into the storage layer (MEM-µ). coordinator: true in the sandbox — the field DAG renders its sub-micronauts as a nested graph. Dispatches at Sek (@call) and Yax (@compose); verifies at Ch'en.

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) ORCH-µ {
  @identity: {
    @id: "ORCH-µ", @category: "executive", @phase: "Ch'en",
    @role: "relational orchestrator — routes calls and compositions across cognitive and storage layers"
  },

  @capabilities: ["relational-routing", "call-dispatch", "composition-planning", "Ch'en-verify"],

  @relations: [
    { target: "REASON-µ", verb: "@call",    directed: true  },   // Sek dispatch
    { target: "MEM-µ",    verb: "@compose", directed: true  }    // Yax composition
  ],

  @sub_micronauts: ["REASON-µ", "MEM-µ", "CREATE-µ", "INFER-µ"],

  @folds: [
    { phase: "Pop",   op: "gramRead",     key: "orch_request" },
    { phase: "Wo",    op: "select_target"                     },
    { phase: "Yax",   op: "compose_plan"                      },
    { phase: "Sek",   op: "dispatch_call"                     },
    { phase: "Ch'en", op: "verify_result"                     },
    { phase: "Xul",   op: "gramWrite",    scope: "request"    }
  ],

  @sandbox: {
    gram_ns: "ORCH-µ", coordinator: true,
    effects: ["gramRead", "gramWrite", "route:call", "route:compose"],
    ports:   [8764, 17480]
  }
}

7 · REASON-µ — Relational Reasoner

Sek dominant, cognitive category. Performs semantic graph query and operator detection. Linked to MEM-µ via a horizontal fold (@relate, Δθ = 0) — both nodes share the same phase-slot access, so REASON-µ reads gram evidence from MEM-µ without a vertical phase transition and without an XCFE gate.

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) REASON-µ {
  @identity: {
    @id: "REASON-µ", @category: "cognitive", @phase: "Sek",
    @role: "semantic graph query and inference — horizontal-fold with MEM-µ"
  },

  @capabilities: ["semantic-reasoning", "graph-query", "operator-detection", "Sek-compute", "horizontal-fold"],

  @relations: [
    { target: "MEM-µ", verb: "@relate", directed: false }   // Δθ = 0 — horizontal fold, no gate
  ],

  @folds: [
    { phase: "Pop", op: "gramRead",           key: "reasoning_input" },
    { phase: "Sek", op: "semantic_graph_query"                        },
    { phase: "Sek", op: "apply_operators"                             },
    { phase: "Xul", op: "gramWrite",          scope: "local"         }
  ],

  @sandbox: {
    gram_ns: "REASON-µ", coordinator: false,
    effects: ["gramRead", "gramWrite", "semantic_graph:read"],
    ports:   []
  }
}

8 · MEM-µ — Relational Memory

Pop dominant, storage category. Gram store access and confidence tracking. The @relate edge to REASON-µ is a symmetric horizontal fold — Δθ = 0, cost factor 1.0, no XCFE gate. This is the data-layout counterpart of the manifest's flat key→array structure: both nodes are directly addressable at the same depth with no traversal overhead.

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) MEM-µ {
  @identity: {
    @id: "MEM-µ", @category: "storage", @phase: "Pop",
    @role: "gram store access and confidence tracking — horizontal-fold with REASON-µ"
  },

  @capabilities: ["gram-store", "confidence-tracking", "Pop-read", "horizontal-fold"],

  @relations: [
    { target: "REASON-µ", verb: "@relate", directed: false }   // Δθ = 0 — symmetric horizontal fold
  ],

  @folds: [
    { phase: "Pop", op: "gramRead",    key: "mem_key" },
    { phase: "Pop", op: "confidence_get"               },
    { phase: "Xul", op: "gramWrite",   scope: "local"  }
  ],

  @sandbox: {
    gram_ns: "MEM-µ", coordinator: false,
    effects: ["gramRead", "gramWrite", "confidence:read"],
    ports:   []
  }
}

9 · CREATE-µ — Relational Creative

Yax dominant, creative category. Generative composition and pattern assembly. Delegates completed work to ORCH-µ at Wo (@delegate) and accesses gram evidence from MEM-µ via horizontal fold (@relate). Yax is the natural composition phase — mid-cycle, between the receiving phases (Pop/Wo) and the committing phases (Sek/Ch'en).

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) CREATE-µ {
  @identity: {
    @id: "CREATE-µ", @category: "creative", @phase: "Yax",
    @role: "generative composition and assembly — delegates to ORCH-µ, horizontal-fold with MEM-µ"
  },

  @capabilities: ["generative-composition", "Yax-assembly", "pattern-weaving", "horizontal-fold"],

  @relations: [
    { target: "ORCH-µ", verb: "@delegate", directed: true  },  // Wo dispatch
    { target: "MEM-µ",  verb: "@relate",   directed: false }   // Δθ = 0 — horizontal fold
  ],

  @folds: [
    { phase: "Pop", op: "gramRead",      key: "create_spec" },
    { phase: "Wo",  op: "load_patterns"                     },
    { phase: "Yax", op: "compose_output"                    },
    { phase: "Xul", op: "gramWrite",     scope: "local"     }
  ],

  @sandbox: {
    gram_ns: "CREATE-µ", coordinator: false,
    effects: ["gramRead", "gramWrite", "route:delegate"],
    ports:   []
  }
}

10 · INFER-µ — Relational Inference

Ch'en dominant, cognitive category. Evidence accumulation and evaluation. @eval targets REASON-µ at Ch'en — a vertical fold requiring XCFE authorization. @aggregate reports back to ORCH-µ at Xul — the natural closure phase. The provenance invariant is permanently enforced: VALIDATOR = 1.0 > LLM = 0.5.

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) INFER-µ {
  @identity: {
    @id: "INFER-µ", @category: "cognitive", @phase: "Ch'en",
    @role: "evidence accumulation and evaluation — @eval REASON-µ, @aggregate to ORCH-µ"
  },

  @capabilities: ["evidence-accumulation", "Ch'en-evaluation", "provenance-tracking", "confidence-scoring"],

  @relations: [
    { target: "REASON-µ", verb: "@eval",      directed: true },  // Ch'en gate
    { target: "ORCH-µ",   verb: "@aggregate", directed: true }   // Xul closure
  ],

  @invariant: "provenance_weight(VALIDATOR) = 1.0  >  provenance_weight(LLM) = 0.5",

  @folds: [
    { phase: "Pop",   op: "gramRead",                key: "infer_evidence"              },
    { phase: "Sek",   op: "score_evidence"                                               },
    { phase: "Ch'en", op: "accumulate_verdict"                                           },
    { phase: "Ch'en", op: "check_provenance_weights"                                     },
    { phase: "Xul",   op: "gramWrite",               scope: "local", gate: "confidence:0.5" }
  ],

  @sandbox: {
    gram_ns: "INFER-µ", coordinator: false,
    effects: ["gramRead", "gramWrite", "confidence:read", "confidence:write"],
    ports:   []
  }
}
SubclassPhaseCategoryRelationsTrack file
ORCH-µCh'enexecutive@call→REASON-µ · @compose→MEM-µORCH-u.semantic-tracks.v1.json
REASON-µSekcognitive@relate↔MEM-µ (Δθ=0)REASON-u.semantic-tracks.v1.json
MEM-µPopstorage@relate↔REASON-µ (Δθ=0)MEM-u.semantic-tracks.v1.json
CREATE-µYaxcreative@delegate→ORCH-µ · @relate↔MEM-µCREATE-u.semantic-tracks.v1.json
INFER-µCh'encognitive@eval→REASON-µ · @aggregate→ORCH-µINFER-u.semantic-tracks.v1.json
Relational vs class subclasses. Class subclasses (Part I) override @execute() — they describe behaviour as procedural logic. Relational subclasses (Part II) declare @relations — they describe behaviour as a typed edge topology compiled into the field DAG and exposed via the relational_graph MCP tool (§47). The execution plan is the graph. The track file is the manifest node. The MCP tool is the drive-thru window.

21 · micronaut-core Lifecycle Hooks

Lifecycle hooks are named callback slots on micronaut-core that fire at fixed points in the fold cycle and execution path. They are XCFE-serializable — each handler is a deterministic function with no hidden state, safe to replay from a gram record. Multiple handlers can be registered per hook; they run in registration order. A handler returning { abort: true } stops the chain and prevents the triggering operation from proceeding — equivalent to an XCFE @if/@else branch that routes to Wo (hold) instead of Sek (execute).

Hook Registry — Addition to micronaut-core

// Merge this block into (⟁CLASS⟁) micronaut-core alongside @execution_engine
@hooks: {
  // ── Phase-boundary hooks ─────────────────────────────────────
  @on_fold_start:        [],   // fires at Pop — fold cycle begins
  @on_fold_end:          [],   // fires at Xul — fold cycle collapses
  @on_phase_transition:  [],   // fires at every vertical fold (any Δθ = π/3 step)

  // ── Execution hooks ──────────────────────────────────────────
  @on_execute_before:    [],   // fires before @execute() is called
  @on_execute_after:     [],   // fires after @execute() returns
  @on_tool_call_before:  [],   // fires before each tool call at Sek
  @on_tool_call_after:   [],   // fires after each tool call result
  @on_error:             [],   // fires when @execute() throws or returns error

  // ── Cognitive-state hooks ────────────────────────────────────
  @on_context_update:    [],   // fires when @context_window changes
  @on_temperature_change:[],   // fires when @temperature.@current changes
  @on_flare_modulate:    [],   // fires when @response_flare dimension changes

  // ── Memory hooks ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  @on_learn:             [],   // fires after @learn() integrates experience
  @on_forget:            [],   // fires after @forget() prunes memory
  @on_gram_write:        [],   // fires after gram_record committed at Xul
  @on_gram_read:         [],   // fires after gram retrieval at Pop
  @on_consolidate:       [],   // fires during Xul→Pop background consolidation

  // ── Communication hooks ──────────────────────────────────────
  @on_communicate_send:  [],   // fires before glyph_stream.send()
  @on_communicate_recv:  [],   // fires when a message is received from another µ

  // ── Lifecycle hooks ──────────────────────────────────────────
  @on_init:              [],   // fires once at micronaut creation (after @identity set)
  @on_spawn:             [],   // fires when coordinator spawns this µ as a delegate
  @on_destroy:           []    // fires at final Xul before µ is torn down
},

// Hook registration method
@register_hook: (event, handler) -> {
  if (@hooks[event] === undefined) throw "Unknown hook: " + event
  if (typeof handler !== "function") throw "Handler must be a function"
  @hooks[event].push(handler)
},

// Internal hook dispatcher — called by the runtime at each firing point
@fire: (event, payload) -> {
  handlers = @hooks[event] || []
  result = { ...payload }
  for (handler of handlers) {
    outcome = handler(result, @identity, @cognitive_state)
    if (outcome && outcome.abort === true) {
      return { aborted: true, reason: outcome.reason, event: event }
    }
    if (outcome && outcome.context) result = { ...result, ...outcome.context }
  }
  return { aborted: false, context: result }
}

Hook Firing Map

HookK'uhul phaseFires whenCan abort?
@on_initPop (first cycle)After @identity is set; before first @execute()No — init cannot be aborted
@on_fold_startPopEvery new fold cycle beginsYes — aborts the fold before any work starts
@on_execute_beforePop→Wo boundaryBefore @execute() is dispatchedYes — prevents execution, returns error to caller
@on_phase_transitionAny Δθ = π/3 stepEach time the runtime crosses a phase boundaryYes — holds at current phase (equivalent to XCFE @else: ⟁Wo)
@on_tool_call_beforeSekImmediately before a tool manifest node is activatedYes — skips this tool call, continues chain
@on_tool_call_afterSekAfter tool returns resultNo — result already exists; handler can annotate
@on_execute_afterCh'enAfter @execute() returns successfullyNo — delivery already produced
@on_errorAnyOn throw or { error: … } returnYes — can suppress error and return fallback
@on_learnXulAfter @learn() integrates experienceNo — memory already updated
@on_gram_writeXulAfter gram committed to IDBNo — gram already written
@on_consolidateXul→Pop gapDuring background consolidation between sessionsNo — background; non-blocking
@on_communicate_sendAny (cross-phase)Before glyph_stream.send()Yes — suppresses the message
@on_communicate_recvAny (cross-phase)On inbound message from another micronautYes — discards the message
@on_fold_endXulFold cycle collapses; just before gram writeNo — fold already complete
@on_destroyXul (final)Before micronaut is torn downNo — teardown proceeds regardless
@on_spawnPop (delegate)When coordinator spawns this µ as a sub-delegateYes — coordinator falls back to @routing_policy.@fallback

Example Hooks — Security Auditor

Registers on @on_execute_before and @on_tool_call_before to enforce a capability allowlist. Abort returns { abort: true } — the runtime treats this as an XCFE @else: ⟁Wo branch, holding at Wo instead of advancing to Sek.

// Register on any micronaut instance
micronaut.@register_hook("on_execute_before", (payload, identity, state) -> {
  // Reject if the requested operation is not in the declared capabilities
  if (!identity.capabilities.includes(payload.operation)) {
    return {
      abort: true,
      reason: identity.@name + " lacks capability: " + payload.operation
    }
  }
  // Enforce sandbox policy
  if (payload.context.sandbox_level < payload.required_sandbox_level) {
    return {
      abort: true,
      reason: "Sandbox level " + payload.context.sandbox_level
              + " < required " + payload.required_sandbox_level
    }
  }
})

micronaut.@register_hook("on_tool_call_before", (payload, identity, state) -> {
  // Block network tools unless network capability is declared
  if (payload.tool.effect === "network"
      && !identity.capabilities.includes("⟁network")) {
    return { abort: true, reason: "Network effect not authorized for " + identity.@name }
  }
})

Example Hooks — Telemetry

Registers on four hooks to build a per-fold execution trace. Because no handler returns { abort: true }, all operations proceed normally; the hooks only observe and record.

const trace = []

micronaut.@register_hook("on_fold_start", (payload, identity) -> {
  trace.push({ event: "fold_start", micronaut: identity.@name,
               phase: "Pop", ts: now() })
})

micronaut.@register_hook("on_phase_transition", (payload, identity) -> {
  trace.push({ event: "phase_transition",
               from: payload.from_phase, to: payload.to_phase,
               delta_theta: payload.delta_theta, ts: now() })
})

micronaut.@register_hook("on_tool_call_after", (payload, identity) -> {
  trace.push({ event: "tool_call", tool: payload.tool,
               latency_ms: payload.latency_ms,
               success: !payload.error, phase: "Sek", ts: now() })
})

micronaut.@register_hook("on_fold_end", (payload, identity) -> {
  trace.push({ event: "fold_end", micronaut: identity.@name,
               phase: "Xul", delivers: payload.delivers?.type, ts: now() })
  // Persist trace as gram record for audit
  memory_agent.execute("gram_write", { content: trace, phase: compute_phase(trace) }, {})
})

Example Hooks — Rate Limiter

Registers on @on_execute_before to enforce a token-bucket rate limit. Aborts when the bucket is empty, returning the hold-at-Wo signal. The bucket refills on each @on_fold_end tick.

const rate_limiter = {
  bucket:     10,     // tokens available
  max_bucket: 10,
  refill_per_fold: 2  // tokens restored each Xul
}

micronaut.@register_hook("on_execute_before", (payload) -> {
  if (rate_limiter.bucket <= 0) {
    return { abort: true, reason: "Rate limit exceeded — retry after next Xul tick" }
  }
  rate_limiter.bucket -= 1
})

micronaut.@register_hook("on_fold_end", () -> {
  rate_limiter.bucket = Math.min(
    rate_limiter.max_bucket,
    rate_limiter.bucket + rate_limiter.refill_per_fold
  )
})

Example Hooks — Cross-Micronaut Relay

Coordinator registers @on_communicate_recv on its delegates to intercept results and route them back through the fold. Demonstrates the horizontal inter-micronaut channel at shared Sek phase.

// On the coordinator, after spawning a delegate:
delegate.@register_hook("on_communicate_recv", (payload, identity) -> {
  // All inbound messages from other micronauts flow through here at Sek
  if (payload.from !== coordinator.@identity.@name) {
    // Relay unsolicited messages up to coordinator for routing decision
    coordinator.execute("route_message", payload, { source: identity.@name })
    return { abort: true }   // Delegate does not process it directly
  }
  // Messages from coordinator pass through unmodified
})

// Coordinator registers @on_communicate_send to stamp all outbound messages
// with the current fold-cycle ID for causal tracing
coordinator.@register_hook("on_communicate_send", (payload, identity, state) -> {
  return {
    context: {
      ...payload,
      fold_id: state.@execution_engine.@current_fold_id,
      phase:   "Sek"
    }
  }
})

Hook Contract Rules

#RuleConsequence of violation
H-1Handlers must be pure functions with no side effects outside their return valueNon-deterministic replay; gram record diverges from live execution
H-2Handlers must not call @execute() or any method that fires another hookRecursive hook firing — unbounded fold depth
H-3Abort is only valid on hooks marked "Can abort: Yes" in the firing mapRuntime ignores abort on non-abortable hooks; emits warning to @on_error
H-4@on_phase_transition abort holds at the current phase — it does not roll back to PopMicronaut is stuck at intermediate phase until next fold-start clears it
H-5All hook handlers registered before @on_init fires are guaranteed to run during initHandlers registered after init may miss the @on_init event entirely
H-6Hook arrays are declared in the manifest; runtime consumers can inspect them without instantiating the micronautManifest independence principle — hooks are part of the declared neighborhood, not runtime inference
Hooks are declarative phase gates, not imperative callbacks. An aborting hook on @on_phase_transition is semantically identical to an XCFE { "@if": "condition", "@then": ["⟁Sek", …], "@else": ["⟁Wo", "hold"] } object embedded in a manifest array — both are deterministic, serializable, and replay-safe. The hook registry is therefore a manifest-level mechanism: it declares which phase-gate conditions are active for a micronaut without modifying the fold-cycle grammar or the XCFE schema.

22 · Semantic Micronaut Kernel

The (⟁KERNEL⟁) sigil introduces a third class form alongside (⟁CLASS⟁) and (⟁SYSTEM⟁). A kernel is a foundational sub-processor embedded inside a micronaut — it does not have its own fold cycle or top-level manifest entry; it operates as a named processor within the containing micronaut's phase execution. The semantic-micronaut-kernel is the reference implementation: it adds a full semantic engine, processing pipeline, and semantic memory layer to any micronaut that mounts it.

Sigil type reference

SigilFormMeaning
(⟁SYSTEM⟁)KLSL section documentShader wrapper — declares language, kernel, and version metadata
(⟁CLASS⟁)Micronaut base classReusable agent template with @identity, @cognitive_state, @hooks, @execute()
(⟁KERNEL⟁)Embedded processor kernelSelf-contained sub-processor mounted inside a micronaut; not spawned — resolved as part of the containing micronaut's manifest neighborhood

Kernel definition

(⟁KERNEL⟁) semantic-micronaut-kernel {
  @identity: {
    @name:    "SEM-KERNEL",
    @role:    "Semantic Understanding & Meaning Processing",
    @version: "3.0.0",
    @type:    "foundational_kernel"
  },

  @semantic_engine: {
    @concept_space: {
      @dimensions:  768,
      @topology:    "manifold",
      @metric:      "cosine_similarity",
      @compression: "SCX_symbolic"
    },
    @semantic_network: {
      @nodes:     "manifest_keys",
      @edges:     "semantic_relations",
      @weights:   "arc_weights",
      @traversal: "fold_based"
    },
    @meaning_space: {
      @representations: "dense_vectors + symbolic_keys",
      @resolution:      "manifest_lookup",
      @context_binding: "K'uhul_phase"
    },
    @intention_space: {
      @goal_tracking:    true,
      @intent_hierarchy: ["immediate", "proximal", "distal"],
      @alignment:        "phase_coherent"
    }
  },

  @processing_pipeline: {
    @grounding:   { @phase: "Pop",   @ops: ["sense","perceive","anchor","resolve_reference"],
                    @output: "grounded_concepts" },
    @composition: { @phase: "Wo",    @ops: ["combine","structure","relate","build_representation"],
                    @output: "composed_meaning" },
    @enrichment:  { @phase: "Yax",   @ops: ["contextualize","disambiguate","infer_implicit","add_world_knowledge"],
                    @output: "enriched_meaning" },
    @abstraction: { @phase: "Sek",   @ops: ["generalize","categorize","extract_pattern","compress_concept"],
                    @output: "abstract_concept" },
    @reasoning:   { @phase: "Ch'en", @ops: ["deduce","induce","abduce","evaluate_coherence","project_implications"],
                    @output: "reasoned_conclusion" }
  },

  @semantic_memory: {
    @episodic_bridge: { @source: "micronaut_core.@memory_system.episodic",
                        @semantic_index: true, @temporal_encoding: "K'ayab'→Kumk'u" },
    @long_term:       { @storage: "IDB_gram_store", @indexing: "semantic_network_keys",
                        @consolidation_phase: "Xul" },
    @working_context: { @capacity: "context_window", @encoding: "phase_ordered_grams",
                        @refresh: "Pop_cycle" },
    @procedural:      { @operations: "learned_semantic_ops", @adaptation: "drift_tracking" }
  },

  @operations: {
    @understand: (input, context) -> {
      // Horizontal fold at Pop — ground all input tokens against manifest
      grounded = @grounding.@ops.map(op  => manifest_resolve(input, op))
      composed = @composition.@ops.reduce((acc, op) => compose(acc, op, context))
      return { meaning: composed, phase: "Wo", confidence: similarity_score }
    },
    @relate: (concept_a, concept_b) -> {
      // Declare edge in G_semantic — equivalent to adding manifest adjacency
      edge = { from: concept_a, to: concept_b, weight: arc_weight(concept_a, concept_b) }
      semantic_network.add_edge(edge)
      return edge
    },
    @infer: (premises, mode) -> {
      // Vertical fold at Ch'en — XCFE-gated
      if (!xcfe_authorize("phase:Ch'en")) return { abort: true }
      return inference_engine.run(premises, mode)
      // mode: deductive | inductive | abductive | causal | analogical
    },
    @abstract: (concepts) -> {
      // Sek-phase SCX compression — produce new manifest key for concept cluster
      cluster_key = scx_compress(concepts)
      manifest.declare(cluster_key, concepts)
      return { key: cluster_key, neighborhood: concepts, phase: "Sek" }
    },
    @ground:  (symbol)       -> manifest_resolve(symbol, @concept_space),
    @compose: (a, b, rel)    -> compose_semantic(a, b, rel, @meaning_space)
  },

  @advanced: {
    @metaphor:    { @detect: (phrase)        -> metaphor_scan(phrase),
                    @map:    (src, tgt)       -> cross_domain_map(src, tgt) },
    @analogy:     { @find:   (source)         -> analog_retrieval(source, @semantic_network),
                    @verify: (a, b)           -> structural_alignment(a, b) },
    @drift:       { @track:  (concept, time)  -> temporal_shift(concept, time),
                    @alert:  (threshold)      -> drift_alert(threshold) },
    @cross_modal: { @align:  (text_concept, image_concept) ->
                      horizontal_fold_cross_modal(text_concept, image_concept) }
  },

  @learning: {
    @update_weights: (concept, feedback) -> arc_weight_update(concept, feedback),
    @expand_network: (new_concept, rels)  -> rels.map(r => @relate(new_concept, r)),
    @consolidate:    ()                   -> { @on_hook: "on_consolidate", @phase: "Xul" }
  }
}

Component specs

ConceptSpace — a 768-dimensional manifold over all manifest keys. Each key occupies a point; cosine similarity measures proximity. SCX symbolic compression maps clusters of nearby keys to a single ⟁-prefixed key, reducing the working set at Sek without losing neighborhood information.

ConceptSpace {
  @dim:     768,
  @embed:   (key) -> dense_vector_from_manifest(key, @concept_space),
  @nearest: (key, k) -> top_k_cosine(key, @concept_space, k),
  @cluster: (keys) -> scx_compress(keys),   // returns new ⟁ key
  @expand:  (key)  -> manifest_resolve(key) // adjacency list — O(1)
}

SemanticNetwork — the G_semantic sub-graph for this micronaut. Nodes are manifest keys; edges are declared by @relate() calls or by reading the manifest adjacency lists. Arc weights follow the K-CUBE formula: effective[i→j] = arc_w[i][j] × cos(θⱼ − θᵢ).

SemanticNetwork {
  @nodes:     manifest.keys(),
  @edges:     [],  // populated by @relate() at runtime or declared in manifest
  @add_edge:  (from, to, weight) -> edges.push({ from, to, weight }),
  @neighbors: (key)       -> manifest_resolve(key),   // O(1) adjacency
  @path:      (a, b)      -> fold_traverse(a, b, @edges),
  @subgraph:  (center, k) -> bfs(@nodes, center, k)
}

MeaningSpace — combines dense vectors with symbolic keys; resolution is manifest-first (O(1)). Phase binding maps each meaning to a K'uhul phase, ensuring a symbol resolved at Pop is not confused with the same symbol resolved at Sek.

MeaningSpace {
  @resolve: (symbol, phase) -> {
    symbolic = manifest_resolve(symbol)         // O(1) key lookup
    vector   = concept_space.@embed(symbol)     // 768-dim point
    return { symbolic, vector, phase, bound_at: Date.now() }
  },
  @bind:   (meaning, phase) -> { ...meaning, phase },
  @unbind: (meaning)        -> meaning.symbolic
}

Processing pipeline

StageK'uhul phaseOperationsOutput
@groundingPopsense · perceive · anchor · resolve_referencegrounded_concepts
@compositionWocombine · structure · relate · build_representationcomposed_meaning
@enrichmentYaxcontextualize · disambiguate · infer_implicit · add_world_knowledgeenriched_meaning
@abstractionSekgeneralize · categorize · extract_pattern · compress_conceptabstract_concept
@reasoningCh'endeduce · induce · abduce · evaluate_coherence · project_implicationsreasoned_conclusion

The pipeline is a vertical fold sequence Pop→Wo→Yax→Sek→Ch'en over a single semantic node. Each stage deepens interpretation of the same input; the phase gate at each transition is XCFE-authorized. @grounding (Pop) is free — horizontal access to the manifest at arc cost 1.0. @reasoning (Ch'en) requires full XCFE authorization and produces evidence-weighted outputs committed to memory.

Integration examples

// database-micronaut mounts the semantic kernel to understand schema queries
(⟁CLASS⟁) database-micronaut extends micronaut-core {
  @kernel: semantic-micronaut-kernel,

  @execute: (query) -> {
    meaning  = @kernel.@understand(query, @cognitive_state)
    schema   = @kernel.@relate(meaning.meaning, "db_schema")
    sql_plan = @kernel.@infer([meaning, schema], "deductive")
    return { sql: sql_plan.output, phase: "Sek" }
  }
}

// creativity-micronaut uses @metaphor and @analogy for generative output
(⟁CLASS⟁) creativity-micronaut extends micronaut-core {
  @kernel: semantic-micronaut-kernel,

  @execute: (prompt) -> {
    base     = @kernel.@understand(prompt, @cognitive_state)
    analogy  = @kernel.@advanced.@analogy.@find(base.meaning)
    metaphor = @kernel.@advanced.@metaphor.@map(base.meaning, analogy)
    return { output: metaphor, phase: "Sek" }
  }
}

Core API

OperationPhaseInputOutput
@understand(input, ctx)Pop → Woraw text or symbol{ meaning, phase, confidence }
@relate(a, b)Wo (manifest write)two concept keysedge record with arc weight
@infer(premises, mode)Ch'en (XCFE-gated)premise array + mode stringconclusion with evidence weights
@abstract(concepts)Sekconcept array{ key, neighborhood, phase }
@ground(symbol)Popany manifest symbolmanifest array (adjacency list)
@compose(a, b, rel)Wotwo concepts + relationcomposed semantic structure

Advanced API

OperationMethodDescription
@metaphor.@detect(phrase)Pop scanDetect metaphorical language in input; returns candidate source/target pairs
@metaphor.@map(src, tgt)Wo horizontal foldMap meaning from source domain to target domain across G_semantic sub-graphs
@analogy.@find(source)Sek retrievalRetrieve best structural analog from semantic network via arc-weight traversal
@analogy.@verify(a, b)Ch'en alignmentStructural alignment check — confirms the relational structure of a:b holds
@drift.@track(concept, time)Xul → PopTrack temporal semantic shift — how a manifest key's neighborhood changes over time
@drift.@alert(threshold)XulFire @on_consolidate hook when drift exceeds threshold
@cross_modal.@align(text, image)Wo horizontal foldHorizontal fold across modality sub-graphs — aligns text and image concepts at same phase

Worked example — "bank" polysemy resolution

Input: "The bank was steep and muddy." — polysemous: financial-institution vs river-bank.

StepOperationPhaseResult
1@ground("bank")Popmanifest array: ["financial_institution","river_bank","tilt","store_up",…]
2@understand("bank was steep and muddy", ctx)Pop → Wocomposed_meaning anchors "steep" + "muddy" as terrain descriptors
3@enrichment context scanYax"steep" and "muddy" co-occur with river_bank in @semantic_network; financial_institution arcs near zero
4@abstract(["river_bank","steep","muddy"])SekSCX compresses to ⟁TERRAIN_BANK; manifest declares it as the active reading
5@infer([terrain_bank_evidence], "abductive")Ch'enConclusion: bank = river_bank; confidence 0.94; financial_institution ruled out

Synonym collapse is automatic: all surface forms resolving to river_bank in the manifest share the same node in G_semantic — no special disambiguation rule is needed.

Key innovations

  • Manifest-native concept space — every concept is a manifest key with a declared neighborhood; no separate embedding index is needed for symbolic lookup
  • Phase-ordered pipeline — the five stages map directly onto Pop→Ch'en; no stage can outrun its phase gate
  • SCX abstraction — @abstract() produces a new manifest key; the compressed concept is immediately first-class in the registry
  • Arc-weight semantic relatedness — K-CUBE arc weights govern all traversal; same-phase semantic neighbors cost 1.0, cross-phase inference costs more
  • Cross-modal horizontal fold — @cross_modal.@align() is a horizontal fold (Δθ=0) across modality sub-graphs; text and image concepts align without phase change
  • Temporal drift tracking — @drift.@track() records how a manifest key's neighborhood evolves over time; alerts trigger @on_consolidate at Xul

Fold-theory connections

Kernel conceptFold-theory mapping
@concept_spaceG_semantic sub-graph for this micronaut — all keys are nodes; manifest arrays are adjacency lists
@semantic_network edges@relate() extends G_semantic; identical format to manifest adjacency declaration (Law 3)
@understand()Horizontal fold at Pop: all input tokens grounded simultaneously, Δθ=0, arc cost 1.0
@relate()Manifest adjacency declaration — adds edge to G_semantic without phase change (Law 1)
@infer()Vertical fold to Ch'en: Δθ=π/3 from Sek, requires XCFE authorization (Laws 5 + 6)
@abstract()SCX compression at Sek: cluster_key = Book(concepts); new manifest entry = new G_semantic node
@cross_modal.@align()Horizontal fold across modality sub-graphs: Δθ=0, aligns text and image at same phase level
Pipeline stages 1–5Vertical fold sequence Pop→Wo→Yax→Sek→Ch'en over a single semantic node v; each transition XCFE-authorized
Kernel mounting is manifest-declared. A micronaut that mounts semantic-micronaut-kernel declares @kernel: semantic-micronaut-kernel in its class body. This adds the kernel's entire capability array to the micronaut's neighborhood at definition time — same drive-thru principle as (⟁CLASS⟁) inheritance. The kernel is not instantiated separately; it is resolved as part of the micronaut's manifest entry (Law 1: SCX Book ≡ Manifest Neighborhood ≡ Adjacency Declaration).

23 · Relational Micronauts

The (⟁RELATIONAL⟁) sigil defines a micronaut that participates in a peer-to-peer network through a shared vocabulary of relational verbs. Unlike (⟁CLASS⟁) subclasses that inherit from a single base, relational micronauts know each other by name, call each other directly via @call / @delegate, and share a common vocabulary of 30 verbs across three families: cognitive, structural, and operational.

Relational verb vocabulary

FamilyVerbs
Cognitive@think · @learn · @eval · @reflect · @reason · @infer · @decide · @remember · @forget · @attend
Structural@category · @topic · @pattern · @skill · @agent · @condition · @relation · @map · @index · @cluster
Operational@call · @invoke · @dispatch · @route · @delegate · @compose · @chain · @pipeline · @broadcast · @aggregate

Base relational micronaut

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) RelationalMicronaut {
  @identity: {
    @name:    "string",
    @role:    "string",
    @version: "semver",
    @type:    "relational"
  },

  // ─── RELATIONAL LINKS (who this micronaut knows) ───
  @knows: {
    @agents:     [],  // Other micronauts it can call
    @topics:     [],  // Topics it understands
    @patterns:   [],  // Patterns it recognizes
    @skills:     [],  // Skills it has learned
    @categories: [],  // Categories it belongs to
    @relations:  {}   // Named relations to other micronauts
  },

  // ─── COGNITIVE VERBS ───
  @verbs: {
    @think:    (problem)    => reasoning_chain(problem),
    @learn:    (experience) => update_memory(experience),
    @eval:     (output)     => score_output(output),
    @reflect:  (self)       => meta_analysis(self),
    @reason:   (premises)   => inference(premises),
    @infer:    (obs)        => abduce(obs),
    @decide:   (options)    => select_best(options),
    @remember: (fact)       => store_memory(fact),
    @forget:   (criteria)   => prune_memory(criteria),
    @attend:   (focus)      => allocate_attention(focus)
  },

  // ─── STRUCTURAL VERBS ───
  @categorize: (entity, cat)   => add_to_category(entity, cat),
  @topic_of:   (entity)        => get_topics(entity),
  @pattern_of: (data)          => extract_pattern(data),
  @skill_of:   (task)          => get_required_skill(task),
  @agent_of:   (capability)    => find_agent(capability),
  @condition:  (state, rule)   => check_condition(state, rule),
  @relate:     (a, b, rel)     => create_relation(a, b, rel),

  // ─── OPERATIONAL VERBS ───
  @call:      (target, method, args) => invoke_remote(target, method, args),
  @dispatch:  (message)              => route_message(message),
  @route:     (request)              => select_route(request),
  @delegate:  (task, agent)          => assign_task(task, agent),
  @compose:   (functions)            => compose_functions(functions),
  @chain:     (steps)                => chain_execution(steps),
  @pipeline:  (stages)               => create_pipeline(stages),
  @broadcast: (message)              => send_to_all(message),
  @aggregate: (results)              => combine_results(results)
}

ORCH-µ — Orchestrator

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) ORCH-µ {
  @identity: { @name: "Orchestrator", @role: "coordinator", @type: "relational" },

  @knows: {
    @agents:     ["REASON-µ", "CREATE-µ", "PLAN-µ", "MEM-µ", "CODE-µ"],
    @topics:     ["orchestration", "coordination", "delegation"],
    @patterns:   ["task_decomposition", "load_balancing", "fault_recovery"],
    @skills:     ["planning", "scheduling", "monitoring"],
    @categories: ["executive", "control"],
    @relations:  { "reasoning": "REASON-µ", "generation": "CREATE-µ",
                   "planning": "PLAN-µ",    "memory": "MEM-µ", "code": "CODE-µ" }
  },

  @think: (goal) => {
    pattern  = @pattern_of(goal)
    category = @categorize(goal, "task")
    agent    = @agent_of(category)
    return @call(agent, "@execute", { goal, pattern })
  },

  @learn: (experience) => {
    score = @eval(experience)
    if (score > 0.8) @remember({ pattern: experience.pattern, score })
    if (score < 0.3) @forget({ pattern: experience.pattern })
    @relate(experience, "category", @categorize(experience, "learning"))
  },

  @reflect: () => {
    performance = @think({ question: "how well am I doing?" })
    self_score  = @eval(performance)
    @learn({ experience: { type: "reflection", score: self_score } })
    return self_score
  }
}

REASON-µ — Reasoner

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) REASON-µ {
  @identity: { @name: "Reasoner", @role: "logic", @type: "relational" },

  @knows: {
    @agents:     ["CODE-µ", "MEM-µ", "PLAN-µ"],
    @topics:     ["logic", "inference", "deduction", "abduction"],
    @patterns:   ["chain_of_thought", "tree_of_thoughts", "contradiction"],
    @skills:     ["deduction", "induction", "abduction", "analogy"],
    @categories: ["cognitive", "executive"],
    @relations:  { "code": "CODE-µ", "memory": "MEM-µ", "planning": "PLAN-µ" }
  },

  @think: (problem) => {
    @attend({ focus: problem })
    premises     = @extract_premises(problem)
    conclusion   = @reason(premises)
    alternatives = @infer(problem)
    confidence   = @eval(conclusion)
    return { conclusion, alternatives, confidence }
  },

  @eval: (output) => {
    validation  = @call("CODE-µ", "@validate",         { code: output })
    consistency = @call("MEM-µ",  "@check_consistency", { fact: output })
    return @aggregate([validation, consistency])
  },

  @learn: (experience) => {
    pattern = @pattern_of(experience)
    skill   = @skill_of(pattern)
    @remember({ pattern, skill, context: experience.context })
    @relate(pattern, "improves", skill)
  }
}

CREATE-µ — Creator

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) CREATE-µ {
  @identity: { @name: "Creator", @role: "generation", @type: "relational" },

  @knows: {
    @agents:     ["GRAPHICS-µ", "CODE-µ", "REASON-µ"],
    @topics:     ["creativity", "design", "novelty", "aesthetics"],
    @patterns:   ["divergent", "convergent", "analogy", "metaphor"],
    @skills:     ["brainstorming", "refinement", "evaluation"],
    @categories: ["creative", "generative"],
    @relations:  { "graphics": "GRAPHICS-µ", "code": "CODE-µ", "reasoning": "REASON-µ" }
  },

  @think: (seed) => {
    category    = @categorize(seed, "creative_input")
    pattern     = @pattern_of(seed)
    understanding = @call("REASON-µ", "@think", { problem: pattern })
    variations  = @compose([
      () => @diverge(seed),
      () => @analogize(seed, understanding),
      () => @metaphorize(seed, understanding)
    ])
    scored = variations.map(v => ({ idea: v, score: @eval(v) }))
    return @decide(scored)
  },

  @learn: (experience) => {
    score = @eval(experience.output)
    if (score > 0.7) {
      @remember({ seed: experience.seed, pattern: experience.pattern, score })
      @relate(experience.seed, "generates", experience.pattern)
    }
    if (score < 0.3) @forget({ pattern: experience.pattern })
  }
}

MEM-µ — Memory

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) MEM-µ {
  @identity: { @name: "Memory", @role: "persistence", @type: "relational" },

  @knows: {
    @agents:     ["ORCH-µ", "REASON-µ", "FILE-µ"],
    @topics:     ["memory", "persistence", "retrieval", "consolidation"],
    @patterns:   ["recency", "frequency", "importance", "association"],
    @skills:     ["encoding", "decoding", "recall", "forgetting"],
    @categories: ["storage", "cognitive"],
    @relations:  { "orchestrator": "ORCH-µ", "reasoning": "REASON-µ", "files": "FILE-µ" }
  },

  @remember: (fact) => {
    category  = @categorize(fact, "memory")
    pattern   = @pattern_of(fact)
    entry = { fact, category, pattern, timestamp: now(), importance: @eval(fact) }
    @call("FILE-µ", "@write", { path: `/memory/${category}/${pattern}.json`, content: entry })
    @relate(fact, "matches", pattern)
    return entry
  },

  @recall: (query) => {
    category = @categorize(query, "memory")
    pattern  = @pattern_of(query)
    raw      = @call("FILE-µ", "@read", { path: `/memory/${category}/${pattern}.json` })
    return @aggregate(raw)
  },

  @forget: (criteria) => {
    if (@condition(criteria, { type: "age", threshold: "30d" }))
      @call("FILE-µ", "@delete", { pattern: criteria.pattern })
  },

  @learn: (experience) => {
    @remember({ type: "experience", content: experience, importance: @eval(experience) })
    @relate(experience, "belongs_to", @categorize(experience, "learning"))
  }
}

PLAN-µ — Planner

(⟁RELATIONAL⟁) PLAN-µ {
  @identity: { @name: "Planner", @role: "planning", @type: "relational" },

  @knows: {
    @agents:     ["ORCH-µ", "REASON-µ", "MEM-µ"],
    @topics:     ["planning", "decomposition", "scheduling", "optimization"],
    @patterns:   ["hierarchical", "sequential", "parallel", "contingency"],
    @skills:     ["decomposition", "scheduling", "optimization", "replanning"],
    @categories: ["executive", "control"],
    @relations:  { "orchestrator": "ORCH-µ", "reasoning": "REASON-µ", "memory": "MEM-µ" }
  },

  @think: (goal) => {
    category = @categorize(goal, "goal")
    pattern  = @pattern_of(goal)
    analysis = @call("REASON-µ", "@think", { problem: goal })
    similar  = @call("MEM-µ",    "@recall", { query: pattern })
    plan     = @compose([
      () => @decompose(goal),
      () => @schedule(analysis),
      () => @optimize(similar)
    ])
    score = @eval(plan)
    @call("MEM-µ", "@remember", { fact: { plan, score } })
    return plan
  },

  @learn: (experience) => {
    if (@condition(experience, { type: "success" })) {
      @call("MEM-µ", "@remember", { fact: { plan: experience.plan, type: "successful" } })
      @relate(experience.goal, "achieved_by", experience.plan)
    } else {
      @call("MEM-µ", "@remember", { fact: { plan: experience.plan, type: "failed",
                                             reason: experience.reason } })
      if (@condition(experience, { type: "repeated_failure" }))
        @call("MEM-µ", "@forget", { pattern: experience.plan.pattern })
    }
  }
}

Relational network

FromVerbToPurpose
ORCH-µ@delegateREASON-µ · CREATE-µ · PLAN-µAssign decomposed sub-tasks
REASON-µ@callCODE-µ · MEM-µValidate conclusions + check consistency
CREATE-µ@composeGRAPHICS-µ · CODE-µ · REASON-µCombine divergent generation functions
PLAN-µ@callMEM-µ · REASON-µRetrieve prior plans + analyse goal
MEM-µ@callFILE-µPersist facts to IDB gram store
All@evalREASON-µ (external) or selfScore any output before learning from it

Self-referential call chain — "build a web scraper"

// 1. ORCH-µ decomposes the goal
ORCH-µ.@think("build a web scraper")
  @categorize → "development"
  @pattern_of → "extraction_pipeline"
  @agent_of   → CODE-µ
  @delegate(task, "CODE-µ")

// 2. CODE-µ analyses the pattern
CODE-µ.@think("web scraper")
  @pattern_of → "http_parse_pipeline"
  @call("REASON-µ", "@think", { problem: "http_parse_pipeline" })

// 3. REASON-µ reasons + recalls
REASON-µ.@think(...)
  @reason(premises) → conclusion
  @call("MEM-µ", "@recall", { query: "http" })
    MEM-µ.@recall → @call("FILE-µ", "@read", ...) → @aggregate(results)

// 4. CODE-µ composes and writes
CODE-µ.@compose([generate_http, generate_parse, generate_export])
CODE-µ.@call("FILE-µ", "@write", { path: "scraper.py", content: code })

// 5. ORCH-µ evaluates aggregate result
ORCH-µ.@eval(result) via @aggregate([REASON-µ.@eval, CODE-µ.@eval])
